                         BEGIN
                          Vendor_G.SETFILTER("No.",GETFILTER("Vendor Filter"));
                          IF NOT Vendor_G.ISEMPTY THEN
                           Vendor_G.FINDFIRST;
                         END;
                        IF GETFILTER("Item Filter") <> '' THEN
                         BEGIN
                          Item_G.SETFILTER("No.",GETFILTER("Item Filter"));

                           Item_G.FINDFIRST;
                         END;
                        IF GETFILTER("Location Filter") <> '' THEN
                         BEGIN
                          Location_G.SETFILTER(Code,GETFILTER("Location Filter"));
                          IF NOT Location_G.ISEMPTY THEN
                           Location_G.FINDFIRST;
                         END;
                        IF GETFILTER("Campaign No Filter.") <> '' THEN
                         BEGIN
                          Campaign_G.SETFILTER("No.",GETFILTER("Campaign No Filter."));
                          IF NOT Campaign_G.ISEMPTY THEN
                           Campaign_G.FINDFIRST;
                         END;
                        IF GETFILTER("Species No. Filter") <> '' THEN
                         BEGIN
                          Species_G.SETFILTER(Code,GETFILTER("Species No. Filter"));
                          IF NOT Species_G.ISEMPTY THEN
                           Species_G.FINDFIRST;
                         END;
                      END;

        Return ""

    Return Value
